specs/recon/check.py 360 lines · checks every spec file
"""The spec check: python3 specs/recon/check.py, from the repository root. Exit 0 when every spec file passes.

What it proves, and nothing more:
  - every .xml under specs/recon/ (rules.xml, root.xml, tasks/*.xml) is well-formed;
  - every <quote> whose nearest ancestor carries a `path` or `from` is verbatim in that file, runs of white space read
    as one (for a JSON file, in one of its string values);
  - a quoted sentence in a rule's from is verbatim in the file that from names before it; the operator's own words are
    a ruling, not a file, and are not checked;
  - no text or attribute writes a count (a number of two or more digits outside a date, a version, an issue number,
    a mandate's §, a table's number, a gate's id or a fallback attribute), a credential (a token's shape), a path of the
    operator's own home, or the word reader;
  - every file a spec names in a reference attribute (path, file, from, reflects, builds, where) exists, unless a task
    of the sequence builds or removes it, and none is a prose document (a README, CLAUDE.md, a page, docs/), the
    authority files BLUEPRINT.md, SUPERX_SCHEMA.md and the skill's SKILL.md apart;
  - no spec carries its history: no changelog, no version-by-version header; a task file's header, branch and PR title
    carry its version attribute;
  - every rule has its from, text and breach; every decision is confirmed, with its date and where, or open, with the
    task of the sequence before whose file it closes, and no file of that task or of a later one exists while it is
    open;
  - the root's read step leaves what is no source to authority not_sources; what a PR holds is listed in the root's
    method pr step alone; the root's specs and repository elements name every entry the repository tracks there;
  - every task file has the shape every task file has: start, piece, before, after, reflects, reads, build with
    modules and numbered steps and a tests element whose every prove names a step the file has, agent-decides, gates
    each with id, kind (local or live), proves, cmd and expect, owns, forbid, done; the root's task of the same id
    names the file and the same issue; a task's comparison attribute is yes or absent.
A spec file is committed only when this check passes on it. specs/recon/mutate_check.py plants one defect of each
class above and proves this check names it.
"""
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
REPO = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
SPECS = os.path.relpath(HERE, REPO)

TASK_SHAPE = ("start", "piece", "before", "after", "reflects", "reads", "build", "agent-decides", "gates", "owns", "forbid", "done")
TASK_ATTRS = ("id", "name", "issue", "version", "date", "reflects", "builds")
GATE_KINDS = ("local", "live")

NUMBER = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.\-/:#§])\d[\d,]*\d(?![\w.\-/:])")   # two or more digits: a 0 or a 1 is a relation, not a count
EXEMPT = (
    re.compile(r"\b\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d\b"),                 # a date
    re.compile(r"\bv?\d+(?:\.\d+)+\b"),                 # a version
    re.compile(r"#\d+"),                                # an issue
    re.compile(r"§\d+"),                                # a mandate of the skill
    re.compile(r"\b[A-Z]\d+\b"),                        # an id: a gate, a phase, a decision of the blueprint
    re.compile(r"\btables? \d+(?:\s*(?:,|–|-|to|and)\s*\d+)*"),   # a table of the structure, by its number
    re.compile(r"\bsteps? \d+(?:\s*(?:,|–|-|to|and)\s*\d+)*"),    # a step of a task file, by its number
    re.compile(r"\b(?:mode|chmod) 0?[0-7]{3}\b"),          # a file's mode
    re.compile(r"\bHTTP [1-5]\d\d\b"),                    # an HTTP status
)
CREDENTIAL = re.compile(r"\b(?:ghp|gho|ghu|ghs|ghr)_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|\bgithub_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{16,}|\bATATT[A-Za-z0-9_\-=]{16,}|\bxox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}")
HOME_PATH = re.compile(r"/(?:Users|home)/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+")
READER = re.compile(r"(?<![\w-])readers?\b(?!-)", re.I)
BANS_READER = "crawler-is-the-reader"   # the rule and the decision that ban the word say it
PATH_TOKEN = re.compile(r"(?<![\w.<-])((?:[\w.-]+/)*[\w<>*-]+(?:\.[\w-]+)*\.(?:xml|json|py|rs|surql|toml|md|html|tsx|ts)|(?:[\w.-]+/)+)(?![\w.])")
REF_ATTRS = ("path", "file", "from", "reflects", "builds", "where")
PROSE = re.compile(r"\.html$|(?:^|/)README|CLAUDE\.md$|^docs/", re.I)
AUTHORITY_PROSE = ("BLUEPRINT.md", "SUPERX_SCHEMA.md", ".claude/skills/zero-trust-execution/SKILL.md")
PR_PHRASES = ("PR body", "into the PR", "in the PR")


def norm(s):
    return " ".join(s.split())


def json_strings(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for v in obj.values():
            yield from json_strings(v)
    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        for v in obj:
            yield from json_strings(v)
    elif isinstance(obj, str):
        yield obj


def holds(path, quote):
    """None when the file at path holds the quote, verbatim, runs of white space read as one; else why not."""
    full = os.path.join(REPO, path)
    if not os.path.isfile(full):
        return f"{path} does not exist"
    text = open(full, encoding="utf-8").read()
    q = norm(quote)
    if path.endswith(".json"):
        return None if any(q in norm(s) for s in json_strings(json.loads(text))) else f"{path} holds no string containing: {q[:60]}"
    return None if q in norm(text) else f"{path} does not contain: {q[:60]}"


def scan(text, where, problems, reader=True):
    stripped = text
    for rx in EXEMPT:
        stripped = rx.sub(" ", stripped)
    for m in NUMBER.finditer(stripped):
        problems.append(f"{where}: a count written down: {m.group(0)}")
    for m in CREDENTIAL.finditer(text):
        problems.append(f"{where}: a credential written down: {m.group(0)[:8]}…")
    for m in HOME_PATH.finditer(text):
        problems.append(f"{where}: a path of the operator's own home: {m.group(0)}")
    for m in (READER.finditer(re.sub(r"`[^`]*`", "", text)) if reader else ()):   # a word in backquotes is named, not used
        problems.append(f"{where}: the word {m.group(0)}: the crawler is the reader (rules crawler-is-the-reader)")


def tracked(prefix):
    """The paths the repository tracks under prefix, so a local leftover is not counted."""
    try:
        return subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files", prefix], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.split()
    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError):
        return []


def built(root, task_roots):
    """What the tasks build or remove: paths that may be absent, before their task is merged or after it."""
    out = set()
    for t in (list(root.iter("task")) if root is not None else []) + task_roots:
        for p in (t.get("builds") or "").split() + (t.get("removes") or "").split():
            out.add(p)
        if t.get("file"):
            out.add(t.get("file"))
    return out


def exists(token):
    import glob
    pattern = re.sub(r"<[^>]*>", "*", token)
    full = os.path.join(REPO, pattern)
    return bool(glob.glob(full)) or os.path.exists(full)


def references(rel, root, built_paths, problems):
    """Every file a spec names in a reference attribute exists, unless a task builds or removes it, and none is prose."""
    for el in root.iter():
        for attr in REF_ATTRS:
            for token in PATH_TOKEN.findall(el.get(attr) or ""):
                where = f"{rel} <{el.tag} {attr}>"
                if PROSE.search(token) and token not in AUTHORITY_PROSE:
                    problems.append(f"{where}: a prose document as a source: {token}")
                elif not exists(token) and not any(token == b or token.startswith(b) for b in built_paths):
                    problems.append(f"{where}: names {token}, which does not exist")


def rules_from(rel, root, problems):
    """A quoted sentence in a rule's from is verbatim in the file named before it; the operator's words are not checked."""
    for rule in root.iter("rule"):
        text = norm(rule.findtext("from") or "")
        markers = sorted([(m.start(), m.group(1)) for m in PATH_TOKEN.finditer(text) if not m.group(1).endswith("/")]
                         + [(m.start(), None) for m in re.finditer(r"(?i)\bthe operator\b", text)])
        for m in re.finditer(r'"([^"]+)"', text):
            before = [src for pos, src in markers if pos < m.start()]
            source = before[-1] if before else "?"
            if source is None:
                continue
            if source == "?" or holds(source, m.group(1)):
                problems.append(f"{rel} rule {rule.get('id')}: from quotes a sentence {source} does not hold: {m.group(1)[:60]}")


def rules_whole(rel, root, problems):
    for rule in root.iter("rule"):
        for part in ("from", "text", "breach"):
            if not norm(rule.findtext(part) or ""):
                problems.append(f"{rel} rule {rule.get('id')}: no {part}")


def history(rel, raw, root, problems):
    if root.find("changelog") is not None:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: carries a changelog; the history of a spec file is its git log")
    for m in re.finditer(r"<!--(.*?)-->", raw, re.S):
        if re.search(r"\bVersion \d+ \(", m.group(1)):
            problems.append(f"{rel}: its header comment carries a version-by-version history; the history is the git log")


def decisions(root, problems):
    tasks = root.find("sequence").findall("task")
    order = [t.get("id") for t in tasks]
    for d in root.iter("decision"):
        status = d.get("status")
        if status == "confirmed":
            if not d.get("date") or not d.get("where"):
                problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml decision {d.get('id')}: confirmed, with no date or no where")
        elif status == "open":
            task = d.get("closes-before")
            if task not in order:
                problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml decision {d.get('id')}: open, and closes-before names no task of the sequence")
                continue
            for later in tasks[order.index(task):]:
                if later.get("file") and os.path.exists(os.path.join(REPO, later.get("file"))):
                    problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml decision {d.get('id')}: open, and closes before {task}, while {later.get('file')} exists")
                    break
        else:
            problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml decision {d.get('id')}: status is {status}; it is confirmed or open")


def read_step(root, problems):
    read = norm(root.findtext("method/read") or "")
    if "not_sources" not in read:
        problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml <read>: does not leave what is no source to authority not_sources")
    for phrase in ("earlier spec", "other branch", "conversation", "outside the repository", "memory"):
        if phrase in read:
            problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml <read>: repeats not_sources ({phrase}); what is no source is listed in not_sources alone")


def pr_once(root, problems):
    pr = root.find("method/pr")
    for el in root.iter():
        for phrase in PR_PHRASES:
            if el is not pr and phrase in (el.text or ""):
                problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml <{el.tag}>: says what goes {phrase}; what a PR holds is listed in method pr alone")


def coverage(root, problems):
    """The root's specs and repository elements name every entry the repository tracks there."""
    for tag in ("specs", "repository"):
        el = root.find(f"identity/{tag}")
        if el is None or not el.get("path"):
            continue
        base = el.get("path").rstrip("/")
        entries = set()
        for p in tracked(base + "/"):
            head, sep, _ = p[len(base) + 1:].partition("/")
            entries.add(head + sep)
        for e in sorted(entries):
            if e.startswith(".") or e == "__pycache__/":
                continue
            if not re.search(r"(?<![\w./-])" + re.escape(e) + ("" if e.endswith("/") else r"(?![\w.-])"), el.text or ""):
                problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml <{tag}>: does not name {base}/{e}, which the repository holds")


def shape(rel, raw, root, sequence, problems):
    """A task file's shape, its version in its header, branch and title, its gates and its tests, its place in the sequence."""
    for tag in TASK_SHAPE:
        if root.find(tag) is None:
            problems.append(f"{rel}: no <{tag}>")
    for attr in TASK_ATTRS:
        if not root.get(attr):
            problems.append(f"{rel}: no {attr} attribute")
    if root.get("comparison") not in (None, "yes"):
        problems.append(f"{rel}: comparison is {root.get('comparison')}; it is yes or absent")
    v = root.get("version")
    start = root.findtext("start") or ""
    if f"-v{v}-" not in start or f" v{v} · " not in start:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: the start element's branch or PR title does not carry version {v}")
    if f"File version {v} " not in raw:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: the header does not say file version {v}")
    modules = root.findall("build/module")
    if not modules:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: no <module> under <build>")
    steps = {s.get("n") for s in root.iter("step") if s.get("n")}
    for m in modules:
        if not list(m.iter("step")):
            problems.append(f"{rel}: module {m.get('path')} has no <step>")
    tests = root.find("build/tests")
    if tests is None or not tests.findall("prove"):
        problems.append(f"{rel}: no <tests> with <prove> items under <build>")
    else:
        for p in tests.findall("prove"):
            if p.get("step") not in steps | {"platform", "prints"}:
                problems.append(f"{rel}: a prove names step {p.get('step')}, which the file does not have")
    gates = root.findall("gates/gate")
    if not gates:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: no <gate>")
    for g in gates:
        for attr in ("id", "kind", "proves"):
            if not g.get(attr):
                problems.append(f"{rel}: gate {g.get('id') or '?'} has no {attr}")
        if g.get("kind") not in GATE_KINDS:
            problems.append(f"{rel}: gate {g.get('id')} kind is not local or live")
        if g.find("cmd") is None or g.find("expect") is None:
            problems.append(f"{rel}: gate {g.get('id')} has no cmd or no expect")
    task = sequence.get(root.get("id"))
    if task is None:
        problems.append(f"{rel}: the root's sequence has no task {root.get('id')}")
    else:
        if task.get("issue") != root.get("issue"):
            problems.append(f"{rel}: issue {root.get('issue')} but the root says {task.get('issue')}")
        if os.path.basename(task.get("file", "")) != os.path.basename(rel):
            problems.append(f"{rel}: the root's task {root.get('id')} names the file {task.get('file')}")


def main():
    problems = []
    files = sorted(p for d in (HERE, os.path.join(HERE, "tasks")) if os.path.isdir(d)
                   for p in (os.path.join(d, f) for f in os.listdir(d)) if p.endswith(".xml"))
    root_xml, sequence = None, {}
    try:
        root_xml = ET.parse(os.path.join(HERE, "root.xml")).getroot()
        sequence = {t.get("id"): t for t in root_xml.find("sequence").findall("task")}
    except Exception as e:
        problems.append(f"{SPECS}/root.xml: no sequence to check the task files against: {e}")
    task_roots = []
    for f in files:
        try:
            r = ET.parse(f).getroot()
            if r.tag == "task":
                task_roots.append(r)
        except ET.ParseError:
            pass
    built_paths = built(root_xml, task_roots)
    quotes = 0
    for f in files:
        rel = os.path.relpath(f, REPO)
        try:
            root = ET.parse(f).getroot()
        except ET.ParseError as e:
            problems.append(f"{rel}: not well-formed: {e}")
            continue
        raw = open(f, encoding="utf-8").read()
        if root.tag == "task":
            shape(rel, raw, root, sequence, problems)
        if root.tag == "rules":
            rules_from(rel, root, problems)
            rules_whole(rel, root, problems)
        if root.tag == "root":
            decisions(root, problems)
            read_step(root, problems)
            pr_once(root, problems)
            coverage(root, problems)
        references(rel, root, built_paths, problems)
        history(rel, raw, root, problems)
        for m in re.finditer(r"<!--(.*?)-->", raw, re.S):
            scan(m.group(1), f"{rel} <!-- header -->", problems)
        parents = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in p}

        def bans_reader(el):
            while el is not None:
                if el.get("id") == BANS_READER:
                    return True
                el = parents.get(el)
            return False

        for el in root.iter():
            where = f"{rel} <{el.tag}>"
            attrs = " ".join(v for k, v in el.attrib.items() if k not in ("path", "from", "fallback", "epic", "issue", "version", "date", "n", "step"))
            scan((el.text or "") + " " + attrs, where, problems, reader=not bans_reader(el))
            if el.tag == "quote":
                quotes += 1
                p = el
                while p is not None and not (p.get("path") or p.get("from")):
                    p = parents.get(p)
                if p is None:
                    problems.append(f"{where}: a quote with no file to check against")
                    continue
                err = holds(p.get("path") or p.get("from"), el.text or "")
                if err:
                    problems.append(f"{where}: {err}")
    if problems:
        print("\n".join(problems))
        print(f"check: {len(problems)} problem(s) in {len(files)} file(s)")
        return 1
    print(f"check ok: {len(files)} spec file(s), {quotes} quote(s) verbatim; no count, credential, home path or reader "
          "written down; every reference exists and none is prose; no history; every rule whole; every decision dated "
          "or before its task; the read step, the PR and the coverage of the root; every task file whole")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

specs/recon/mutate_check.py 76 lines · proves the check catches
"""The mutation test of the spec check: python3 specs/recon/mutate_check.py [checkout], from the repository root.

It plants one defect of each class specs/recon/check.py guards, one at a time, runs the check, proves the check fails
naming it, and restores the file before the next; it ends by running the check on the restored files. It rewrites
files in place while it runs, so it runs in a worktree of its own, never in a build folder. Exit 0 when every defect
is caught.
"""
import os
import subprocess
import sys

W = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".."))
ROOT, RULES, T01 = "specs/recon/root.xml", "specs/recon/rules.xml", "specs/recon/tasks/536.xml"
PROBE = "specs/recon/tasks/t09-probe.xml"
BARE_TASK = '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<task id="t09" name="probe" issue="" version="1" date="2026-10-26">\n  <piece>x</piece>\n</task>\n'

# (label, file, old, new, what the check must say); a case whose old is None creates the file with new as its text.
cases = [
    ("authority quote altered", ROOT, "<quote>Nothing here is judged.</quote>", "<quote>Nothing here is ever judged.</quote>", "does not contain: Nothing here is ever judged."),
    ("rules quote altered", RULES, '"Facts go in columns; judgments do not."', '"Facts go in columns; judgments never do."', "does not hold: Facts go in columns; judgments never do."),
    ("quote with no file", ROOT, "<product>The models", "<product><quote>loose</quote>The models", "a quote with no file to check against"),
    ("count written", ROOT, "Five tasks: the structure first", "Five tasks, the 42 PRs: the structure first", "a count written down: 42"),
    ("count in an attribute", ROOT, 'command="superx-recon run" builds="crates/superx-mod-recon/src/crawler/lines/"', 'command="superx-recon run 15" builds="crates/superx-mod-recon/src/crawler/lines/"', "a count written down: 15"),
    ("credential", ROOT, "<not_sources>Earlier", "<not_sources>ghp_abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwx Earlier", "a credential written down"),
    ("home path", ROOT, "the folder its executables are installed in.", "the folder its executables are installed in, /Users/someone/bin.", "a path of the operator's own home: /Users/someone"),
    ("reader", ROOT, "One crawler crawls every line", "One reader crawls every line", "the word reader"),
    ("stale reference", ROOT, '<file path="SUPERX_SCHEMA.md"', '<file path="SUPERX_SCHEMA_V3.md"', "names SUPERX_SCHEMA_V3.md, which does not exist"),
    ("prose source", ROOT, '<file path="BLUEPRINT.md"', '<file path="README.md"', "a prose document as a source: README.md"),
    ("changelog", ROOT, "  </decisions>\n</root>", '  </decisions>\n  <changelog><entry date="2026-10-26" version="0">x</entry></changelog>\n</root>', "carries a changelog"),
    ("header history", ROOT, "  Parts: identity, authority, method, sequence, decisions.", "  Parts: identity, authority, method, sequence, decisions.\n  Version 1 (2026-10-27): what changed.", "version-by-version history"),
    ("rule without breach", RULES, '    <breach>review</breach>\n  </rule>\n\n  <rule id="specs-are-the-truth">', '  </rule>\n\n  <rule id="specs-are-the-truth">', "rule trunk: no breach"),
    ("decision undated", ROOT, '<decision id="recon" status="confirmed" date="2026-10-26"', '<decision id="recon" status="confirmed"', "decision recon: confirmed, with no date or no where"),
    ("decision status", ROOT, '<decision id="recon" status="confirmed"', '<decision id="recon" status="pending"', "status is pending"),
    ("open decision with no task", ROOT, '<decision id="recon" status="confirmed"', '<decision id="recon" status="open" closes-before="t09"', "closes-before names no task of the sequence"),
    ("read repeats not_sources", ROOT, "Nothing else: nothing authority not_sources names.", "Nothing else: no conversation, nothing authority not_sources names.", "repeats not_sources (conversation)"),
    ("PR content outside pr", ROOT, "<deliverable>A task delivers code, tests and the PR with its gate table.", "<deliverable>A task delivers code, tests and the PR with its gate table, and the PR body says so.", "says what goes PR body"),
    ("task file shape", PROBE, None, BARE_TASK, "no <start>"),
    ("task outside the sequence", PROBE, None, BARE_TASK, "the root's sequence has no task t09"),
    ("stale version in start", T01, "feat/536-structure-v1-", "feat/536-structure-v9-", "does not carry version 1"),
    ("stale version in header", T01, "File version 1 (2026-10-26)", "File version 9 (2026-10-26)", "the header does not say file version 1"),
    ("gate of no known kind", T01, '<gate id="G6" kind="live"', '<gate id="G6" kind="remote"', "gate G6 kind is not local or live"),
    ("prove naming a step the file lacks", T01, '<prove step="11">', '<prove step="12">', "a prove names step 12"),
    ("issue unlike the root's", T01, 'issue="536" version="1"', 'issue="537" version="1"', "issue 537 but the root says 536"),
    ("count in a task file", T01, "<piece>The structure task.", "<piece>The structure task, its 42 tests.", "a count written down: 42"),
    ("reader in a task file", T01, "takes a name other than `reader`.", "takes a name other than reader.", "the word reader"),
    ("not well-formed", ROOT, "</root>", "</rooot>", "not well-formed"),
]

failed = 0
for label, rel, old, new, expect in cases:
    path = os.path.join(W, rel)
    original, made_dir = None, False
    if old is None:
        if not os.path.isdir(os.path.dirname(path)):
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path))
            made_dir = True
        open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(new)
    else:
        original = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
        assert original.count(old) == 1, (label, original.count(old))
        open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(original.replace(old, new))
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "specs/recon/check.py"], cwd=W, capture_output=True, text=True)
    finally:
        if original is None:
            os.remove(path)
            if made_dir:
                os.rmdir(os.path.dirname(path))
        else:
            open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(original)
    caught = r.returncode == 1 and expect in r.stdout
    failed += not caught
    print(f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'}  {label}" + ("" if caught else f"\n   exit {r.returncode}: {r.stdout[-300:]}"))
r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "specs/recon/check.py"], cwd=W, capture_output=True, text=True)
print("after restore:", r.stdout.strip()[:60], "| exit", r.returncode)
sys.exit(1 if failed or r.returncode else 0)

specs/recon/build_issues.py 85 lines · renders the issues
"""The GitHub issues of the build, rendered from the spec: python3 specs/recon/build_issues.py [task id ...] | --epic

The repository is the source (rules specs-are-the-truth): one issue per task of the root's sequence, its text from the
root and, when the task's file exists, from that file. Nothing flows back from GitHub but the number, which the
operator writes into the task's issue="" and the file's name. The output is JSON, one object per task:
{id, issue, title, body}; the body is Markdown, as gh issue create --body-file takes it.
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = ET.parse(os.path.join(HERE, "root.xml")).getroot()
EPIC = ROOT.get("epic")
VERSION = ROOT.get("version")


def text(el):
    return " ".join((el.text or "").split()) if el is not None else ""


def task_file(task):
    """The task's own file, when it exists: by the file attribute, else by its issue number."""
    for name in (task.get("file", "").split("/")[-1], f"{task.get('issue')}.xml"):
        path = os.path.join(HERE, "tasks", name)
        if name and name != ".xml" and os.path.exists(path):
            return ET.parse(path).getroot(), os.path.relpath(path, os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
    return None, None


def render(task, order):
    spec, path = task_file(task)
    key = f"#{task.get('issue')}" if task.get("issue") else task.get("id")
    version = spec.get("version") if spec is not None else "0"
    name = task.get("name")
    lines = [f"## {order} · {name}", "",
             f"**Runs as:** `{task.get('command')}`  ",
             f"**Reflects:** `{task.get('reflects')}`  ",
             f"**Builds:** `{task.get('builds')}`  ",
             f"**After:** {task.get('depends') or 'nothing: the first task'}  ",
             f"**Spec:** version {VERSION}; {'`' + path + '`' if path else 'the task file is not written yet'}"
             + ("; a comparison across models" if spec is not None and spec.get("comparison") == "yes" else ""), "",
             text(task.find("piece")), ""]
    issue = task.get("issue") or "<issue>"
    lines += [f"**Kickoff:** point a model at `specs/recon/tasks/{issue}.xml`, nothing else; it writes its own label "
              f"`<model>-<effort>` and reads the root, the rules and the merged code itself. Branch "
              f"`feat/{issue}-{name.replace(' ', '-')}-v{version}-<model>-<effort>` from main; PR into main titled "
              f"`#{issue} {name} v{version} · <model>-<effort>`; every commit starts with `#{issue}`.", ""]
    lines += [f"Under epic #{EPIC}.", ""]
    lines += ["> " + text(q) for q in task.findall("quote")]
    if spec is not None:
        gates = spec.findall(".//gate")
        if gates:
            lines += ["", "### Gates", ""] + [f"- **{g.get('id')}** ({g.get('kind')}): {text(g.find('expect'))}" for g in gates]
    lines += ["", "---", "Generated from the repository by specs/recon/build_issues.py. The task's XML file under "
              "specs/recon/tasks/ is the spec; an agent builds from it, specs/recon/root.xml, specs/recon/rules.xml and "
              "the code merged on main, nothing else. The branch and the PR carry this issue's number."]
    return {"id": task.get("id"), "issue": task.get("issue") or None, "title": f"recon {order} · {name}", "body": "\n".join(lines)}


def epic():
    """The epic's text: the product, the spec's place, the sequence with its issues; nothing else."""
    tasks = ROOT.find("sequence").findall("task")
    lines = [f"## SuperX Recon · build spec version {VERSION}", "", text(ROOT.find("identity/product")), "",
             "The build spec lives in the repository under `specs/recon/`: `rules.xml`, `root.xml` and one XML file per "
             "task under `tasks/`, named by its issue. An agent builds one task from its file, the root, the rules and the "
             "code merged on main, nothing else.", "", "### The sequence", ""]
    lines += [f"{i}. {('#' + t.get('issue')) if t.get('issue') else t.get('id')} · {t.get('name')}" for i, t in enumerate(tasks, 1)]
    lines += ["", "---", "Generated from the repository by specs/recon/build_issues.py (--epic)."]
    return {"issue": EPIC, "body": "\n".join(lines)}


def main(argv):
    if argv == ["--epic"]:
        print(json.dumps(epic(), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0
    tasks = ROOT.find("sequence").findall("task")
    wanted = set(argv) or {t.get("id") for t in tasks}
    print(json.dumps([render(t, i) for i, t in enumerate(tasks, 1) if t.get("id") in wanted], indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

specs/recon/build_page.py 354 lines · renders this page
"""The Build Spec page, rendered from the spec files: python3 specs/recon/build_page.py [--whole] [out.html]

One page: the map of the build (the root and every task of the sequence, numbered, in their order), the decisions,
the rules whole, the root whole, every task file whole, and each file's XML as written. Nothing here is written by
hand: every word comes from specs/recon/rules.xml, specs/recon/root.xml and specs/recon/tasks/*.xml.

The page kept in the repository, build_spec.html, holds every file of the map whole but the structure: its DDL lives in
its own file alone, since tools/skill_audit.py (X1) refuses DDL in any other file of the repository. With --whole the
page holds the structure too; that is the page published for review, and it is written outside the repository.
"""
import html
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
REPO = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))

esc = html.escape
RULES = ET.parse(os.path.join(HERE, "rules.xml")).getroot()
ROOT = ET.parse(os.path.join(HERE, "root.xml")).getroot()
TASKS = ROOT.find("sequence").findall("task")


def text(el):
    return " ".join((el.text or "").split()) if el is not None else ""


def task_file(task):
    for name in (task.get("file", "").split("/")[-1], f"{task.get('issue')}.xml"):
        path = os.path.join(HERE, "tasks", name)
        if name and name != ".xml" and os.path.exists(path):
            return path
    return None


def sha():
    try:
        return subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip()
    except Exception:
        return "working copy"


def key(t):
    return f"#{t.get('issue')}" if t.get("issue") else t.get("id")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the map: every box a file of the repository
def slug(path):
    return "src-" + "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "-" for c in path).strip("-")


def authority_paths():
    """The files the root's authority names, in its order: what the root and the rules quote."""
    out = []
    for f in ROOT.find("authority").findall("file"):
        if f.get("path") not in out:
            out.append(f.get("path"))
    return out


def decides(path):
    """What the root says a file decides, to its first colon or comma."""
    for f in ROOT.find("authority").findall("file"):
        if f.get("path") == path:
            d = " ".join((f.get("decides") or "").split())
            for stop in (":", ","):
                d = d.split(stop)[0]
            return d
    return ""


ROW = {"authority": (12, 46), "spec": (172, 58), "task": (322, 58), "tool": (424, 50)}


def map_files():
    """Every file the map draws, by its place: (path, line 1, line 2, x, y, w, h, kind)."""
    files = []
    auth = [p for p in authority_paths() if not p.endswith(".surql")]
    for i, p in enumerate(auth):
        r, c = divmod(i, 3)
        files.append((p, os.path.basename(p), decides(p), 20 + c * 330, ROW["authority"][0] + r * 56, 300, ROW["authority"][1], "authority"))
    structure = next(p for p in authority_paths() if p.endswith(".surql"))
    open_n = sum(1 for d in ROOT.iter("decision") if d.get("status") == "open")
    y, h = ROW["spec"]
    files.append(("specs/recon/rules.xml", "rules.xml", f"{len(RULES.findall('rule'))} rules, each binding every task", 40, y, 220, h, "spec"))
    files.append(("specs/recon/root.xml", "root.xml", f"method, sequence, decisions · {open_n} open", 390, y, 220, h, "root"))
    files.append((structure, "recon.surql", "the structure · no task writes it", 740, y, 240, h, "structure"))
    tw, tgap = 220, (1000 - 40 - 4 * 220) / 3
    y, h = ROW["task"]
    for i, t in enumerate(TASKS):
        path = os.path.relpath(task_file(t), REPO) if task_file(t) else t.get("file")
        sub = f"{key(t)} · {t.get('name')}" + ("" if task_file(t) else " · not written yet")
        files.append((path, f"tasks/{os.path.basename(path)}", sub, 20 + i * (tw + tgap), y, tw, h, "task" if task_file(t) else "missing"))
    tools = [("specs/recon/check.py", "checks every spec file"), ("specs/recon/mutate_check.py", "proves the check catches"),
             ("specs/recon/build_issues.py", "renders the issues"), ("specs/recon/build_page.py", "renders this page")]
    y, h = ROW["tool"]
    for i, (p, sub) in enumerate(tools):
        files.append((p, os.path.basename(p), sub, 20 + i * (tw + tgap), y, tw, h, "tool"))
    return files


def map_svg():
    files = map_files()
    at = {f[0]: f for f in files}
    def mid_x(p):
        f = at[p]
        return f[3] + f[5] / 2
    root, rules = at["specs/recon/root.xml"], at["specs/recon/rules.xml"]
    structure = next(f for f in files if f[7] == "structure")
    tasks = [f for f in files if f[7] in ("task", "missing")]
    auth = [f for f in files if f[7] == "authority"]
    edges, labels = [], []
    frame_bottom = max(f[4] + f[6] for f in auth) + 8
    frame = f'<rect x="8" y="4" width="984" height="{frame_bottom - 4}" rx="12" class="frame"/>'
    edges.append((f"M{mid_x(rules[0]):.0f},{frame_bottom} V{rules[4] - 3}", True))
    edges.append((f"M{mid_x(root[0]):.0f},{frame_bottom} V{root[4] - 3}", True))
    labels.append((mid_x(root[0]) + 10, frame_bottom + 20, "quoted word for word by the root and the rules; check.py proves every quote", "start"))
    edges.append((f"M{root[3]},{root[4] + 29} H{rules[3] + rules[5] + 3}", True))
    labels.append(((root[3] + rules[3] + rules[5]) / 2, root[4] + 22, "read next: the rules", "middle"))
    edges.append((f"M{root[3] + root[5]},{root[4] + 29} H{structure[3] - 3}", True))
    labels.append(((root[3] + root[5] + structure[3]) / 2, root[4] + 22, "its authority", "middle"))
    tbus = (root[4] + root[6] + tasks[0][4]) // 2 + 8
    edges.append((f"M{mid_x(root[0]):.0f},{root[4] + root[6]} V{tbus}", False))
    edges.append((f"M{mid_x(rules[0]):.0f},{rules[4] + rules[6]} V{tbus}", False))
    edges.append((f"M{tasks[0][3] + tasks[0][5] / 2:.0f},{tbus} H{tasks[-1][3] + tasks[-1][5] / 2:.0f}", False))
    for t in tasks:
        edges.append((f"M{t[3] + t[5] / 2:.0f},{tbus} V{t[4] - 3}", True))
    labels.append((mid_x(root[0]) + 8, root[4] + root[6] + 20, "its sequence names each task file", "start"))
    labels.append((mid_x(rules[0]) + 8, rules[4] + rules[6] + 20, "every rule binds every task", "start"))
    edges.append((f"M{tasks[-1][3] + tasks[-1][5] - 30:.0f},{tasks[-1][4]} V{structure[4] + structure[6] + 3}", True))
    labels.append((tasks[-1][3] + tasks[-1][5] - 36, structure[4] + structure[6] + 20, "each task reflects it", "end"))
    for a, b in zip(tasks, tasks[1:]):
        edges.append((f"M{a[3] + a[5]},{a[4] + 29} H{b[3] - 3}", True))
    tools = [f for f in files if f[7] == "tool"]
    labels.append((20, tools[0][4] - 10, "the pack's tools: the check and its mutation test, and the renderers of the issues and this page", "start"))
    height = tools[0][4] + tools[0][6] + 12
    out = [f'<svg class="map" viewBox="0 0 1000 {height}" role="group" aria-label="The spec pack: every file of the repository it is made of, and how each names the others. Each box opens the file as the repository holds it.">',
           '<defs><marker id="arrow" viewBox="0 0 8 8" refX="7" refY="4" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 z" class="mk"/></marker></defs>',
           frame]
    out += [f'<path d="{d}" class="edge"{" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"" if arrow else ""}/>' for d, arrow in edges]
    out += [f'<text x="{x:.0f}" y="{y:.0f}" text-anchor="{anchor}" class="lbl">{esc(t)}</text>' for x, y, t, anchor in labels]
    for path, l1, l2, x, y, w, h, kind in files:
        exists = os.path.exists(os.path.join(REPO, path))
        box = (f'<rect x="{x:.0f}" y="{y}" width="{w}" height="{h}" rx="9" class="box {kind}"/>'
               f'<text x="{x + w / 2:.0f}" y="{y + (22 if h > 52 else 21)}" text-anchor="middle" class="l1">{esc(l1)}</text>'
               f'<text x="{x + w / 2:.0f}" y="{y + (42 if h > 52 else 39)}" text-anchor="middle" class="l2">{esc(l2)}</text>')
        if exists:
            out.append(f'<a href="#{slug(path)}" data-src="{slug(path)}"><title>{esc(path)}: open the file as the repository holds it</title>{box}</a>')
        else:
            out.append(f'<g><title>{esc(path)}: not written yet</title>{box}</g>')
    out.append("</svg>")
    return "".join(out)


def sources_html(whole):
    """Every file the map draws that exists, whole, as the repository holds it: what each box opens. The structure's
    text only when whole: in the repository its DDL lives in its own file alone (tools/skill_audit.py, X1)."""
    out = []
    for path, l1, l2, *_ in map_files():
        full = os.path.join(REPO, path)
        if not os.path.exists(full):
            continue
        body = open(full, encoding="utf-8").read()
        meta = f"{len(body.splitlines())} lines · {l2}"
        if path.endswith(".surql") and not whole:
            body = (f"{path} is not copied into this page: the structure's DDL lives in its own file alone, since "
                    "tools/skill_audit.py (X1) refuses DDL in any other file of the repository. Open the file itself, "
                    "or the page python3 specs/recon/build_page.py --whole renders outside the repository, which holds it whole.")
        out.append(f'<details class="src" id="{slug(path)}" data-path="{esc(path)}" data-meta="{esc(meta)}">'
                   f'<summary><code>{esc(path)}</code> <span class="sub">{esc(meta)}</span></summary><pre>{esc(body)}</pre></details>')
    return "".join(out)


VIEWER = ('<div id="viewer" class="viewer" hidden><div class="vhead"><code class="vpath"></code><span class="vmeta sub"></span>'
          '<button type="button" class="vclose" aria-label="Close the file">Close</button></div><pre class="vbody"></pre></div>')
SCRIPT = """<script>
(function(){
  var viewer = document.getElementById('viewer');
  function show(id){
    var src = document.getElementById(id); if(!src) return false;
    viewer.querySelector('.vpath').textContent = src.getAttribute('data-path');
    viewer.querySelector('.vmeta').textContent = src.getAttribute('data-meta');
    viewer.querySelector('.vbody').textContent = src.querySelector('pre').textContent;
    viewer.hidden = false; viewer.scrollIntoView({block: 'start'});
    return true;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('svg.map a[data-src]').forEach(function(a){
    a.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(show(a.getAttribute('data-src'))) e.preventDefault(); });
  });
  viewer.querySelector('.vclose').addEventListener('click', function(){ viewer.hidden = true; });
})();
</script>"""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- generic XML rendering
def attrs(el, skip=()):
    items = [(k, v) for k, v in el.attrib.items() if k not in skip and v]
    if not items:
        return ""
    return '<dl class="attrs">' + "".join(f"<dt>{esc(k)}</dt><dd>{esc(v)}</dd>" for k, v in items) + "</dl>"


def render(el, depth=2):
    if el.tag == "quote":
        return f"<blockquote>{esc(text(el))}</blockquote>"
    if el.tag == "note":
        return f'<p class="note">{esc(text(el))}</p>'
    title = el.get("id") or el.get("name") or el.tag
    h = min(depth, 5)
    out = [f'<section class="el el-{esc(el.tag)}"><h{h} id="x-{esc(el.tag)}-{esc(title)}">{esc(el.tag)}{"" if title == el.tag else " · " + esc(title)}</h{h}>',
           attrs(el, skip=("id", "name"))]
    if text(el):
        out.append(f"<p>{esc(text(el))}</p>")
    for child in el:
        out.append(render(child, depth + 1))
    out.append("</section>")
    return "".join(out)


def raw(path):
    return (f'<details class="raw"><summary>{esc(os.path.relpath(path, REPO))}, the XML as written</summary>'
            f'<pre>{esc(open(path, encoding="utf-8").read())}</pre></details>')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tables
def table(head, rows):
    return ('<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>' + "".join(f"<th>{esc(h)}</th>" for h in head) + "</tr></thead><tbody>"
            + "".join(rows) + "</tbody></table></div>")


def decisions_html():
    rows = []
    for d in sorted(ROOT.iter("decision"), key=lambda d: d.get("status") != "open"):
        status = d.get("status")
        when = f"closes before {d.get('closes-before')}" if status == "open" else d.get("date", "")
        rows.append(f'<tr class="{esc(status)}"><td><code>{esc(d.get("id"))}</code></td><td><span class="pill {esc(status)}">{esc(status)}</span></td>'
                    f'<td>{esc(when)}</td><td>{esc(text(d))}</td><td>{esc(d.get("where", ""))}</td></tr>')
    return table(("Decision", "Status", "When", "What", "Where it lives"), rows)


def rules_html():
    rows = [f'<tr id="r-{esc(r.get("id"))}"><td><code>{esc(r.get("id"))}</code></td><td>{esc(text(r.find("from")))}</td>'
            f'<td>{esc(text(r.find("text")))}</td><td>{esc(text(r.find("breach")))}</td></tr>' for r in RULES.findall("rule")]
    return table(("Rule", "From", "Text", "Breach caught by"), rows)


def sequence_html():
    rows = []
    for i, t in enumerate(TASKS, 1):
        rows.append(f'<tr><td>{i}</td><td><code>{esc(key(t))}</code></td><td><a href="#t-{esc(t.get("id"))}">{esc(t.get("name"))}</a></td>'
                    f'<td>{esc(text(t.find("piece")))}</td><td><code>{esc(t.get("builds"))}</code></td><td>{esc(t.get("depends") or "")}</td></tr>')
    return table(("#", "Task", "Name", "The piece", "Builds", "After"), rows)


def tasks_html():
    out = []
    for i, t in enumerate(TASKS, 1):
        f = task_file(t)
        head = f"{i} · {key(t)} · {t.get('name')}"
        out.append(f'<section class="task" id="t-{esc(t.get("id"))}"><h3>{esc(head)}</h3>')
        if not f:
            out.append('<p class="note">The task file is not written yet. What the root says of this task:</p>'
                       + attrs(t, skip=("id", "name")) + f"<p>{esc(text(t.find('piece')))}</p>"
                       + "".join(f"<blockquote>{esc(text(q))}</blockquote>" for q in t.findall("quote")) + "</section>")
            continue
        el = ET.parse(f).getroot()
        out.append(attrs(el, skip=("id", "name")))
        for child in el:
            out.append(render(child, 4))
        out.append(raw(f) + "</section>")
    return "".join(out)


CSS = """

def page(whole):
    files = [os.path.join(HERE, "rules.xml"), os.path.join(HERE, "root.xml")] + [f for f in (task_file(t) for t in TASKS) if f]
    body = [f'<h1>Recon Build Spec</h1><p class="sub">Version {esc(ROOT.get("version"))} · epic #{esc(ROOT.get("epic"))} · {esc(ROOT.get("date"))} · rendered from specs/recon/ at {esc(sha())}. '
            "An agent reads the rules, the root, its one task file and the code merged on main, and nothing else.</p>",
            f'<p>{esc(text(ROOT.find("identity/product")))}</p>',
            '<h2 id="s-map">The spec pack</h2><p class="sub">Every box is a file of the repository; open one to read it as the '
            "repository holds it. The arrows are how the files name each other. A build starts at its task file, reads the root, "
            "then the rules, then its task file whole.</p><div class=\"mapw\">" + map_svg() + "</div>" + VIEWER,
            '<h2 id="s-sequence">The sequence</h2><p class="note">' + esc(text(ROOT.find("sequence/note"))) + "</p>" + sequence_html(),
            '<h2 id="s-decisions">The decisions</h2><p class="note">' + esc(text(ROOT.find("decisions/note"))) + "</p>" + decisions_html(),
            '<h2 id="s-the-rules">The rules · specs/recon/rules.xml</h2>' + rules_html() + raw(files[0]),
            '<h2 id="s-the-root">The root · specs/recon/root.xml</h2>']
    for part in ROOT:
        if part.tag not in ("sequence", "decisions"):
            body.append(render(part, 3))
    body.append(raw(files[1]))
    body.append('<h2 id="s-the-tasks">The task files · specs/recon/tasks/</h2>' + tasks_html())
    body.append('<h2 id="s-files">The files, whole</h2><p class="sub">What each box of the map opens: the file as the repository '
                "holds it at this commit.</p>" + sources_html(whole))
    body.append(f'<p class="foot">Rendered by specs/recon/build_page.py from {", ".join(esc(os.path.relpath(f, REPO)) for f in files)}; '
                "the spec check (specs/recon/check.py) passes on them.</p>")
    return ('<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
            '<title>Recon Build Spec</title><meta name="description" content="The build spec of SuperX Recon: the sequence, the decisions, the rules, the root and every task file, rendered from the repository.">'
            f"<style>{CSS}</style></head><body><div class=\"wrap\">" + "".join(body) + "</div>" + SCRIPT + "</body></html>")


def main(argv):
    whole = "--whole" in argv
    rest = [a for a in argv if a != "--whole"]
    out = os.path.abspath(rest[0]) if rest else os.path.join(HERE, "build_spec.html")
    if whole and os.path.commonpath([out, REPO]) == REPO:
        print("build_page.py --whole copies the structure's DDL into the page: write it outside the repository "
              "(tools/skill_audit.py, X1)", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(page(whole))
    print(f"wrote {out if whole else os.path.relpath(out, REPO)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
